There is an iterative deconvolution / Newton method for p*p ≈ t (target flat) with t_j chosen. Since equations quadratic, one can solve for p with active set linearized.

Suppose we choose target vector t_j = M (flat or with endpoints scaled). We need p such that p*p≈t. Since p nonnegative, this is like square root in convolution algebra. We can iteratively approximate:
Given current p, compute b=p*p. Define correction q such that 2 p*q ≈ t-b, i.e., q is deconvolution of error by p. Then update p←p+q. This is Newton's method for solving P^2=T. In polynomial algebra, if P invertible (nonzero constant?), q = T - P^2 convolved with inverse of 2P. In time domain, can use FFT? But deconvolution length. For infinite sequences, zeroth-order? - Temperature schedule: For Adam, we can use weights w = softmax((b - M)/τ?) Standard softmax(b/τ). If τ high, weights more uniform over all b, causing flatten variance; if low, peak. We can do phases:

Let's plan basis optimizer in code:
```
def basis_matrix(n, D):
    x = np.linspace(-1.0,1.0,n) # z_i = 2*i/(n-1)-1
    Z = np.empty((n,D))
    for j in range(D):
       k=2*j+1
       Z[:,j] = np.cos(k*np.arccos(x)) # Chebyshev T_k
    return Z
```
Chebyshev T_k can accumulate error; use recurrence for odd modes up to 17 okay. Or use simple powers z^{2j+1} maybe bounded and orthogonal not; powers amplifying high modes. Chebyshev stable. Use recurrence:
T0=1,T1=x; compute all up to max odd. Z odd columns T_{2j+1}. Good.

Objective for basis:
```
def score_basis(v, frac, D, Z, n, m2, tau_override=None):
    s=Z.dot(v)
    h=np.tanh(s)
    f=1+h
    # Due to symmetric Z/tanh, sum f should n, but normalize
    p=f/np.sum(f)
    b=conv_fft(p,m2,n)
    M=b.max()
    if tau_override is None: tau=frac*M
    w=softmax(b/tau)
    gp=2*grad_corr(w,p,n,m3)
    # grad wrt s
    du=f*(1-h*h) / S
    term = gp - np.dot(p,gp)
    gs=du*term
    gv=Z.T.dot(gs)
    return p,b,M,w,gp,gs? 
```
Memory: Z n×D; Z.T.dot(gs) O(nD). Good.
We can optimize by avoiding building f repeatedly? okay.

```
  conv=np.fft.irfft(fw*fq,m3)
```

Basis optimizer:
```
def _optimize_basis(v0, Z, n, m2, m3, budget_s, rng=None):
    D=len(v0)
    v=v0.copy()
    # Adam state
    m=np.zeros(D); vu=np.zeros(D); t=0
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    lr=0.5
    best_p=None; best_score=inf
    clock=time.monotonic()
    deadline=clock+budget_s
    # tau frac phases over iterations or time
    while clock<deadline:
       frac = ... based on elapsed fraction? use current objective maybe: frac = 0.25 if t<... else ...
       s=Z.dot(v); h=np.tanh(s); f=1+h
       S=f.sum(); p=f/S
       b,m,w = _score_and_weights(p,m2,n,frac)
       score=2*n*m
       if score<best_score: best=...
       gp=_grad_coeff_from_w(w,p,n,m3)
       gs=_grad_s(p,s,h,gp)
       gv=Z.T.dot(gs)
       # Adam
       t+=1
       m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*gv
       vu=beta2*vu+(1-beta2)*gv*gv
       mh=m/(1-beta1**t); vh=vu/(1-beta2**t)
       v -= lr*mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
       # clip v
       v=np.clip(v,-12,12)
       clock=time.monotonic()
    return best_p,best_score
```
But if v saturates, gradient small; okay.
Initial lr? For basis, gradient gv O? If score ~2, gradient maybe ~0.1. lr=0.5 may be okay. But if too large, oscillate. Could use adaptive lr based on previous improvement: if best improves, keep else reduce? Simpler use lr schedule: 0.5 for first phase, 0.2 later, 0.05 smallest? Or use Adam with lr=0.2. def _basis_objective(v, args):
```
    Z,n,m2,m3,frac,bounds = args
    s=Z.dot(v)
    h=np.tanh(s)
    f=1.0+h
    S=f.sum()
    if S<=0: return 1e9, np.zeros_like(v)
    p=f/S
    L=2*n-1
    b=_convolve(p,m2)[:L]
    if b.size==0: ...
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    M=float(b.max())
    if M<=0: return 1e9,...
    tau=max(frac*M, 1e-12)
    x=b/tau; mx=x.max(); e=np.exp(x-mx); w=e/e.sum()
    lse=mx+np.log(e.sum())
    score=float(2.0*n*tau*(lse - np.log(L)))
    r=_corr(w,p,n,m3)
    gp_score=2.0*n*r*2.0? Wait r is sum w p, ∂F_unscaled =2r. score gradient =2n*2r =4n r. But earlier gp=2r. Let's set gp_score = 4.0*n*r.
```

```
def _full_objective(z, args):
    n,m2,m3,frac = args
    zc=z - np.mean(z)
    lp=zc - np.max(zc)
    p=np.exp(lp); S=p.sum()
    if S<=0: return 1e9, np.zeros_like(z)
    p/=S
    ...
    r=_corr(w,p,n,m3)
    gp_score=4.0*n*r
    gl=p*(gp_score - float(np.dot(p,gp_score)))
    return score, gl
```

```
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(1,budget_s)-5
    if budget_s <=20: return best of simple families? 
    n=8192 if budget_s>600 else 4096
    D=12
    Z=_build_cheb(n,D)
    m2=_next_pow2(2*n-1); m3=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    candidates=_basis_candidates(D, seed, n)
    best_p=None; best_score=np.inf
    top_list=[]
    # phase 1: each candidate basis L-BFGS with fracs [0.25,0.1,0.03]
    budgets per cand: allocate at least 15s, total ~ phase1=0.45*avail
    max_cands = min(len(candidates), max(3, int(phase1_time/30)))
    for i,v0 in enumerate(candidates):
       if time>deadline - reserve for full: break
       v_best=v0.copy(); score_best=inf
       v_curr=v_curr
       for frac in (0.25,0.1,0.03,0.008):
           # estimate cand_deadline = min(start + per_frac, deadline - reserve)
           res=minimize(_basis_objective, v_curr, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', args=(Z,n,m2,m3,frac,bounds), options={...})
           if res.fun < score_best: ...
           v_curr=res.x
       p=_basis_p(v_best,...); score=...; update top
       record (score,p,v)
    # sort top, keep best 3/5
    # phase 2: full-domain L-BFGS on top arrays
    for p0 in top:
       if time remains:
          z0=np.log(p0+1e-300)-mean
          for frac in (0.1,0.03,0.005):
             res=minimize(_full_objective, z0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', args=(n,m2,m3,frac), options={... maxiter smaller})
             p=_softmax(res.x); score=...
             update best
             z0=res.x
    # Also evaluate simple initial candidates without optimization? We can include linear p, uniform.
```

```
def _p_score(p,n,m2):
    b=_convolve(p,m2)[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    M=float(b.max())
    return 2.0*n*M, M, b
```

Implement dyn per-candidate time check:
```
def _basis_candidate(v0, Z,n,m2,m3,deadline,reserve_s):
    best_v=v0; best_p=_basis_to_p; best_score=...
    v_curr=v0
    for frac in fracs:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       args=(Z,n,m2,m3,frac,logL)
       try:

          res=minimize(_basis_objective, v_curr, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', args=args, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
       except Exception:
          res=None
       if res is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           v_test=res.x
       else:
           v_test=v_curr
       p=_basis_to_p(v_test); score=...
       if score<best_score: best...
       # For next frac, if v_test improved smooth? Use v_test if score not too bad? Use v_test always if res success, else v_curr
       if res is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)) and res.fun < 1e8:
           v_curr=res.x
       else:
           break
    return best_p,best_score,best_v
```

```
def _smooth_score_weights(b, tau, logL):
    x=b/tau
    mx=x.max()
    e=np.exp(x-mx)
    s=e.sum()
    lse=mx+np.log(s)
    w=e/s
    score=tau*(lse-logL)
    return score, w
```

`_optimize_basis` top-level signature:
```
def _optimize_basis(v0, Z, n, m2, m3, fracs, maxfun, deadline):
    logL=np.log(2*n-1)
    bounds=[(-8,8)]*len(v0)
    best_v=v0.copy(); best_p=_basis_to_p(...); best_score=...
    v_curr=v0.copy()
    for frac in fracs:
       p_curr=_basis_to_p(v_curr); _,M,b? = _p_score_only; tau=max(frac*M,1e-12)
       args=(Z,n,m2,m3,tau,logL)

       except: break/continue
       if res is not None and finite: v_next=res.x else v_next=v_curr
       p_next=_basis_to_p(v_next); s=...
       if s<best_score: best...
       # if res success or fun finite: v_curr = v_next else break
       if finite v_next: v_curr=v_next else break
    return best_p,best_score,best_v
```

```
"""
Uses low-dimensional Chebyshev/tanh parameterization of nonnegative densities, optimizes smooth max convolution with L-BFGS, then refines in full log-space.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

def _top_key(item): return item[0]
...
def propose_candidate(...):
   ...
```

Need ensure `_top_key` returns float. Good.

Let's detail `_basis_objective`:
```
def _basis_objective(v, args):
    Z,n,m2,m3,tau,logL = args
    s=Z.dot(v)
    h=np.tanh(s)
    f=1.0+h
    f=np.where(f>0.0,f,0.0) # or np.maximum
    S=float(np.sum(f))
    D=len(v)
    if S<=0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(D)
    p=f/S
    b=_conv_real(p,m2)
    L=2*n-1
    b=b[:L]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    if float(np.max(b)) <=0:
       return 1e100, np.zeros(D)
    if tau<=0 or not np.isfinite(tau): tau=1e-12
    sc,w=_smooth_score_weights(b,tau,logL)
    score=2.0*n*sc
    if not np.isfinite(score): return 1e100, zeros
    r=_corr(w,p,n,m3)
    gp=4.0*float(n)*r
    mean=float(np.dot(p,gp))
    term=gp-mean
    u=(f*(1.0-h*h))/S
    gs=u*term
    gv=Z.T.dot(gs)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gv)): gv=np.where(np.isfinite(gv),gv,0)
    return float(score), gv
```
If f has zeros where h=-1 and term nonzero, u=0 so gradient zero; okay.
`np.where(f>0,f,0)` creates copy; can use `np.maximum(f,0.0)`.

`_smooth_score_weights` returns score_unscaled,w. If tau fixed, okay.
```
def _smooth_score_weights(b,tau,logL):
    if tau <= 0: tau=1e-15
    x=b/tau
    mx=float(np.max(x))
    e=np.exp(x-mx)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return 1e100, None
    w=e/s
    val=tau*(mx+np.log(s)-logL)
    return val,w
```
If tau extremely small and b all? okay.

`_full_objective`:
```
def _full_objective(z,args):
   n,m2,m3,tau,logL = args
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return 1e100, np.zeros_like(z)
   zm = z - float(np.mean(z))
   lp = zm - float(np.max(zm))
   p = np.exp(lp)
   S=float(np.sum(p))
   if S<=0: return ...
   p/=S
   b=...
   sc,w=...
   score=2n*sc
   r=_corr(w,p,n,m3)
   gp=4*n*r
   mean=dot(p,gp)
   gl=p*(gp-mean)
   gl -= float(np.mean(gl))  # enforce sum zero
   if not finite: gl=0
   return score, gl
```
`np.all(np.isfinite(z))` O(n) each eval, okay. Could skip for speed but safe.

```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

def _top_key(item):
    return float(item[0])

def _next_pow2(x):
   m=1
   while m < int(x): m <<=1
   return m

def _conv_real(p,m):
   f=np.fft.rfft(p,m)
   return np.fft.irfft(f*f,m)

def _smooth_score_weights(b,tau,logL):
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): return 1e100, None
   if tau <=0 or not np.isfinite(tau): tau=1e-15
   x=b/tau
   mx=float(np.max(x))
   e=np.exp(x-mx)
   s=float(np.sum(e))
   if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return 1e100, None
   w=e/s
   val=tau*(mx + np.log(s) - logL)
   return val,w
```

```
    start=time.monotonic()

    reserve=30.0 if total>100 else 5.0
```

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Algorithm:
  1. Work with normalized probabilities p = a/sum(a), so the score is 2n*max(conv(p,p)).
  2. Use a low-dimensional Chebyshev/tanh parameterization to find good smooth
     densities, minimizing a smoothed maximum of the linear convolution.
  3. Refine the best densities in full log-softmax space with L-BFGS-B.
  4. Optionally polish at a slightly larger length and evaluate candidates exactly.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None


def _top_key(item):
    return float(item[0])


def _next_pow2(x):
    m = 1
    x = int(max(1, x))
    while m < x:
        m <<= 1
    return m


def _conv_real(p, m):
    f = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    return np.fft.irfft(f * f, m)


def _smooth_score_weights(b, tau, logL):
    """Return (unscaled smooth score, softmax weights over b)."""
    if tau <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 1e-15
    x = b / tau
    mx = float(np.max(x))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return 1e100, None
    e = np.exp(x - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100, None
    w = e / s
    val = tau * (mx + np.log(s) - logL)
    if val < 0.0:
        val = 0.0
    return float(val), w


def _corr_w_p(w, p, n, m3):
    """
    Compute r_i = sum_j w_j p_{j-i} for i=0..n-1,
    where w has length 2n-1 and p has length n.
    """
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, m3)
    fq = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], m3)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fw * fq, m3)
    r = conv[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    r = np.asarray(r, dtype=np.float64)
    r = np.maximum(r, 0.0)
    return r


def _p_score_only(p, n, m2):
    b = _conv_real(p, m2)[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    M = float(np.max(b))
    return 2.0 * float(n) * M, M


def _basis_to_p(v, Z):
    s = Z.dot(v)
    h = np.tanh(s)
    f = 1.0 + h
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(f))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        n = len(f)
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return f / S


def _z_to_p(z, n):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size != n:
        z = z[:n]
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    lp = z - float(np.max(z))
    p = np.exp(lp)
    S = float(np.sum(p))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / S


def _basis_objective(v, args):
    Z, n, m2, m3, tau, logL = args
    D = len(v)
    bad = (1e100, np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64))

    s = Z.dot(v)
    h = np.tanh(s)
    f = 1.0 + h
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(f))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return bad
    p = f / S

    b = _conv_real(p, m2)[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    if float(np.max(b)) <= 0.0:
        return bad

    sc, w = _smooth_score_weights(b, tau, logL)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return bad
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * sc
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return bad

    r = _corr_w_p(w, p, n, m3)
    gp = 4.0 * float(n) * r
    mean = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    term = gp - mean
    u = (f * (1.0 - h * h)) / S
    gs = u * term
    gv = Z.T.dot(gs)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gv)):
        gv = np.where(np.isfinite(gv), gv, 0.0)
    return float(score), gv.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _full_objective(z, args):
    n, m2, m3, tau, logL = args
    bad = (1e100, np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64))

    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return bad

    zc = z - float(np.mean(z))
    lp = zc - float(np.max(zc))
    p = np.exp(lp)
    S = float(np.sum(p))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return bad
    p = p / S

    b = _conv_real(p, m2)[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    if float(np.max(b)) <= 0.0:
        return bad

    sc, w = _smooth_score_weights(b, tau, logL)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return bad
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * sc
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return bad

    r = _corr_w_p(w, p, n, m3)
    gp = 4.0 * float(n) * r
    mean = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    gl = p * (gp - mean)
    gl -= float(np.mean(gl))

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gl)):
        gl = np.where(np.isfinite(gl), gl, 0.0)
    return float(score), gl.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _cheb_matrix(n, D):
    x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    Z = np.empty((n, D), dtype=np.float64)

    T0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    T1 = x.astype(np.float64, copy=True)

    for k in range(1, D + 1):
        if k == 1:
            Tk = T1
        else:
            Tk = 2.0 * x * T1 - T0
            T0, T1 = T1, Tk
        Z[:, k - 1] = Tk
    return Z


def _cheb_candidates(D, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cands = []

    # One-dimensional skewed starts, including increasingly steep odd shapes.
    a_vals = [0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 1.1, 1.4, 1.8, 2.3, 3.0]
    for a in a_vals:
        v = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        v[0] = float(a)
        cands.append(v)

    # Random Chebyshev combinations. Even modes are stronger than later modes
    # but damped slightly so the search is encouraged to keep useful skew.
    for _ in range(12):
        v = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(D):
            k = j + 1
            scale = 1.0 / (float(k) ** 0.45)
            if k % 2 == 0:
                scale *= 0.7
            v[j] = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) * scale
        if v[0] < 0.0:
            v[0] *= -1.0
        cands.append(v)

    return cands


def _optimize_basis(v0, Z, n, m2, m3, fracs, maxfun, deadline):
    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        p = _basis_to_p(v0, Z)
        score, _ = _p_score_only(p, n, m2)
        return p, score, np.asarray(v0, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    logL = np.log(2 * n - 1)
    D = len(v0)
    bounds = [(-8.0, 8.0)] * D

    p = _basis_to_p(v0, Z)
    best_score, _ = _p_score_only(p, n, m2)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_v = np.asarray(v0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    v_curr = best_v.copy()

    for frac in fracs:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        p_curr = _basis_to_p(v_curr, Z)
        _, M = _p_score_only(p_curr, n, m2)
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        tau = max(0.25 * frac * M, 1e-13)

        args = (Z, n, m2, m3, tau, logL)
        opts = {"maxiter": 900, "maxfun": int(max(50, maxfun)),
                "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-5, "maxls": 50}
        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(_basis_objective, v_curr, jac=True,
                                  method="L-BFGS-B", args=args,
                                  bounds=bounds, options=opts)
        except Exception:
            break

        if not hasattr(res, "x"):
            break
        v_next = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(v_next)):
            break
        v_next = np.clip(v_next, -8.0, 8.0)

        p_next = _basis_to_p(v_next, Z)
        s_next, _ = _p_score_only(p_next, n, m2)
        if np.isfinite(s_next):
            if s_next < best_score:
                best_score = s_next
                best_p = p_next.copy()
                best_v = v_next.copy()
            v_curr = v_next
        else:
            break

    return best_p, best_score, best_v


def _optimize_full(p0, n, m2, m3, fracs, maxfun, deadline):
    p = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size != n:
        p = p[:n]
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    S = float(np.sum(p))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / S

    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        score, _ = _p_score_only(p, n, m2)
        return p, score, np.full(n, 0.0, dtype=np.float64)

    logL = np.log(2 * n - 1)
    z = np.log(p)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))

    score, _ = _p_score_only(p, n, m2)
    best_score = score
    best_p = p.copy()
    z_curr = z.copy()

    for frac in fracs:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        _, M = _p_score_only(_z_to_p(z_curr, n), n, m2)
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        tau = max(0.25 * frac * M, 1e-13)

        args = (n, m2, m3, tau, logL)
        opts = {"maxiter": 900, "maxfun": int(max(100, maxfun)),
                "ftol": 1e-13, "gtol": 1e-6, "maxls": 20}
        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(_full_objective, z_curr, jac=True,
                                  method="L-BFGS-B", args=args,
                                  options=opts)
        except Exception:
            break

        if not hasattr(res, "x"):
            break
        z_next = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if z_next.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(z_next)):
            break

        p_next = _z_to_p(z_next, n)
        s_next, _ = _p_score_only(p_next, n, m2)
        if np.isfinite(s_next):
            if s_next < best_score:
                best_score = s_next
                best_p = p_next.copy()
            z_curr = z_next
        else:
            break

    return best_p, best_score, z_curr


def _upsample_p(p, n_new):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n_new <= p.size:
        p = p[:n_new]
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        S = float(np.sum(p))
        if S > 0.0:
            p = p / S
        return p
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(q))
    if S <= 0.0:
        q = np.full(n_new, 1.0 / n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q = q / S
    return q


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Search and return the best valid coefficient sequence found."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = 35.0 if total > 100.0 else 8.0
    deadline = start + max(1.0, total - reserve)

    # Very short fallback: return a simple valid skewed density.
    if total < 60.0:
        n = 1024
        z = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        f = 1.0 + 1.0 * z
        f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
        p = f / float(np.sum(f))
        out = []
        for x in p:
            out.append(float(x))
        return out

    # Primary optimization length. 4096 gives a good speed/accuracy tradeoff.
    n = 4096
    D = 12
    logL = np.log(2 * n - 1)
    m2 = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    m3 = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    Z = _cheb_matrix(n, D)

    best_score = np.inf
    best_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    basis_best_score = np.inf
    basis_best_v = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    basis_best_v[0] = 0.8

    top = []

    # A few explicit simple starting points, also useful as fallbacks.
    xgrid = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    for a in [0.0, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.2, 1.5, 2.0]:
        f = 1.0 + float(a) * xgrid
        f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
        S = float(np.sum(f))
        if S <= 0.0:
            continue
        p = f / S
        score, _ = _p_score_only(p, n, m2)
        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
            top.append((score, p.copy()))
            if score < basis_best_score:
                basis_best_score = score
                basis_best_v = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
                # Rough tangent analogue of the linear slope.
                basis_best_v[0] = 0.5 * float(a)

    # Main low-dimensional basis optimization.
    cands = _cheb_candidates(D, int(seed))
    max_cands = min(len(cands), 18 if total > 1200.0 else 10)
    basis_fracs = [0.30, 0.10, 0.02]
    basis_maxfun = 1600 if total > 1200.0 else 800

    for v0 in cands[:max_cands]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 250.0:
            break
        p, score, vb = _optimize_basis(v0, Z, n, m2, m3,
                                       basis_fracs, basis_maxfun, deadline)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            continue
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
        if score < basis_best_score:
            basis_best_score = score
            basis_best_v = np.asarray(vb, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        top.append((score, p.copy()))

    print("basis stage best score:", best_score)

    top.sort(key=_top_key)
    top = top[:10]

    # Full-space refinement of the best low-dimensional candidates.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    full_count = 4 if remaining > 500.0 else 2
    full_maxfun = 2800 if remaining > 850.0 else 1200
    full_fracs = [0.12, 0.04, 0.008]

    for i in range(min(full_count, len(top))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 150.0:
            break
        p0 = top[i][1]
        p, score, _ = _optimize_full(p0, n, m2, m3,
                                     full_fracs, full_maxfun, deadline)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            continue
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
        top.append((score, p.copy()))

    print("full refinement best score:", best_score)

    # Optional higher-resolution polish using the best basis vector and best sequence.
    top.sort(key=_top_key)
    top = top[:12]

    n_high = 0
    if (time.monotonic() < deadline - 260.0 and
            np.isfinite(basis_best_score) and
            basis_best_v is not None and len(basis_best_v) == D):
        n_high = 8192
        m2h = _next_pow2(2 * n_high - 1)
        m3h = _next_pow2(3 * n_high - 2)
        Zh = _cheb_matrix(n_high, D)
        high_fracs = [0.15, 0.05, 0.015]
        p, score, vb_high = _optimize_basis(basis_best_v, Zh, n_high, m2h, m3h,
                                            high_fracs, 900, deadline)
        if np.isfinite(score):
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
            top.append((score, p.copy()))
            print("high-n basis best score:", best_score)

    # Interpolate the best low-length sequence to the high length, if useful.
    if n_high > 0 and len(best_p) == n and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        p_int = _upsample_p(best_p, n_high)
        m2h_int = _next_pow2(2 * n_high - 1)
        score_int, _ = _p_score_only(p_int, n_high, m2h_int)
        if np.isfinite(score_int) and score_int < best_score * 1.002:
            top.append((score_int, p_int))

    # Exact-evaluate the best candidates and return the empirically best valid list.
    top.sort(key=_top_key)
    if not top:
        top = [(best_score, best_p.copy())]

    exact_best = np.inf
    exact_seq = None
    evaluated = 0

    for score, p in top:
        if evaluated >= 7:
            break
        if np.isfinite(exact_best) and score > exact_best + 0.02:
            break
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            s = float(np.sum(arr))
            if arr.size == 0 or s <= 1e-8:
                evaluated += 1
                continue
            arr = arr / s
            seq = []
            for x in arr:
                seq.append(float(x))
            ex = evaluate_sequence(seq)
            if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < exact_best:
                exact_best = ex
                exact_seq = seq
        except Exception:
            pass
        evaluated += 1

    if exact_seq is None:
        exact_seq = []
        for x in best_p:
            exact_seq.append(float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, float(x)))))

    print("final exact best score:", exact_best)
    return exact_seq
```
